- Added an explicit macOS GameTest `--offscreen-gl` option restricted to one disabled fresh-process MM6 test. SDL creates a hidden OpenGL window; a test-only platform/window proxy refuses show/raise, cursor and mouse changes, fullscreen and modal boxes. The test draws the scoped first outdoor map and HUD, reads pixels before swap, checks nonblank world/HUD regions and writes a normalized temporary PNG. Null audio keeps the host speakers untouched. No normal new-game or chest guard was opened.
- The first monitored hidden run reached the world renderer with zero onscreen windows/focus change, then aborted on an unsuffixed seven-character MM6 actor sprite prefix. Retained `dsft.bin`/archive aggregate evidence showed 981 eligible prefixes have numbered zero-octant assets and none has an exact unsuffixed entry. MM6 now appends the octant at seven characters; MM7's existing lookup remains. Focused MM6 headless octant check passed **1/1** across 126 samples, with all eight pointers nonnull and no window/focus change. The accepted executable branch itself was not reconstructed.
- The final custom linked arm64 hidden run passed **1/1** across 390 monitor samples, with zero onscreen windows and no focus change. Its private 640×480 PNG was inspected locally: recognizable map/HUD geometry with black party portraits, white ground/water patches and intense red foliage. Portrait loading ran; causes remain unproved. The MM7 `Inventory.Initialization` headless control passed **1/1** across 45 samples without focus/window change; an offscreen wrong-filter invocation was rejected before SDL startup. Parent integration must rebuild and rerun these focused cases. The PNG, raw asset data and private logs remain untracked. See [public packet](../project/MM6_F4_OFFSCREEN_FRAME_CAPTURE.md) and [wiki page](MM6-F4-Offscreen-Render-Capture.md).

## [2026-11-06] correct | MM6 sprite-frame palette and timing
- by: T-Burton-ND via codex
- The retained MM6 `dsft.bin` and pinned GrayFace `SFTItem` schema agree that MM6 uses 16-bit flags at byte 44, light radius at 46, palette ID at 48, frame time at 52 and total time at 54. The inherited MM7-style 32-bit flags shifted those fields. MM6 and MM7 now have distinct 56-byte and 60-byte snapshots; MM7's field positions remain intact. This supersedes earlier palette-zero and static `aframe1` conclusions in this log. The six recorded selection frames each last 8 ticks in a 48-tick cycle. The current 2D HUD still caches only the first frame, so its animation remains a follow-up.
- A source-art check found the first green tree and a red tree both use recorded palette 140; the red foliage is part of the original MM6 art. A temporary first-map draw trace saw 65 visible tree billboards with that palette. A focused headless tree/portrait test passed **2/2** across 78 monitor samples, and the final hidden OpenGL capture passed **1/1** across 73 samples; neither opened an on-screen window or changed focus. A separate MM7 headless inventory control passed **1/1** across 32 samples. The private capture now has green and red trees but a large cyan/red ground or water region remains wrong. PNGs, raw sprite extracts and logs remain untracked. The ordinary MM6 new-game/chest guards remain closed. See [capture packet](../project/MM6_F4_OFFSCREEN_FRAME_CAPTURE.md), [UI blocker packet](../project/MM6_F4_UI_RESOURCE_BLOCKERS.md), and [offscreen wiki page](MM6-F4-Offscreen-Render-Capture.md).

- by: T-Burton-ND via codex
